For the other orientation term b(1-a) (source low target high b? value small) after = (b+δ)(1-(a-δ))=orig +δ(1-a)+δb+δ^2, increases if a high? Could increase the other lag. But max maybe both? If active set includes the other orientation, sum f+g decreases? Sum after linear: first + second = -δ(1-b)-δa + δ(1-a)+δb = -δ(1-b+a) + δ(1-a+b) = -δ[(1-b+a) - (1-a+b)] = -δ(2a-2b)= -2δ(a-b) negative. So sum decreases; max of f and g may still increase if the smaller term increases but remains below. Since max maybe source high term; the other term b(1-a) small, increasing slightly. Good. So equalizing active pairs seems beneficial.

This active smoothing is cheap: each pair move optional exact evaluate O(n^2). If we evaluate every pair, active* n * passes maybe 10*300*?? 3000 evals; each 90k =>270M, okay for seconds? Another technique: "smoothing by convolution" along active lags: h_new = (1-λ)h + λ * average of h shifted by active lags? This equalizes pairs while preserving sum if shifts with zero-padding? Use full pair swaps: for each offset, replace pair by (h_i + h_j)/2 maybe preserves sum per pair and without napping. This can be vectorized: for active offset k, for i=0..n-k-1, new_i = (h_i+h_{i+k})/2, new_{i+k} = same? This would collapse both to average, not preserve individual if overlapping multiple offsets? But operation `h = (h + np.roll? truncated)/2` blurs, sum changes due boundaries. Could then project. Maybe too crude.

Pair swaps from higher to lower are safe.

Let's design final algorithm:
- Initialize multiple candidates:
  * parent (n=300) project/sym.
  * if parent n even: also symmetrized version.
  * interpolate to n=450 or 600 (if budget) using parent or analytic candidates.
  * simple shapes (cosine family optimized with a quick param search over A maybe) at n_current and n_alt. For shape, we can sample 2D parameters (A, B) or just use adaptive.
- For each candidate, run a local optimization pipeline on its n:
  1. `smooth_lbfgs` with alpha schedule [20,200,2000,20000,200000,1000000] maybe time divided. Since many candidates, limit per candidate.
  2. `active_weight_steps` or `active_smoothing`.
  3. `final_pair_search` small deltas for best candidate.
- Track best across different n. If best at different n, can return any n. But if best at n=600, vector long; okay. Ensure return tuple.

Time allocation critical. We cannot run too many candidates fully. Since parent likely good, allocate most time to parent n=300 and high-res n=600.

Potential improvement: Instead of parent n=300, use n=600 from interpolated parent; local search may lower. But local optimizer slower. Maybe first do quick parent 300 to improve, then use optimized parent to init 600/900 and optimize.

Let's estimate time for L-BFGS n=300: gradient ~0.01s? 1000 iter=10s. n=600: grad ~0.05, 500=25s. SLSQP maybe 60s. Active smoothing few seconds. Total under 1100.

We can implement time-based loops not fixed heavy phases.

Detailed functions:

`c5_vec(h)`:

`compute_c5(h)`: float(np.max(c5_vec(h))).

`project_feasible(h, target=None)`:
```
n=len(h); h=np.asarray(h,float); target=n/2.0
h=np.clip(h,0,1)
for _ in range(100):
 s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
 if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
 if diff>0:
   room=1-h
   tot=room.sum()
   if tot<1e-15: break
   # if diff <= tot? yes
   h += (diff/tot)*room
 else:
   tot=s
   if tot<1e-15: break
   h += (diff/tot)*h  # diff negative
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
# final corrective scaling if tiny: if sum not exact, adjust a few elements
s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
if abs(diff)>0:
  idx=np.argsort(h if diff>0 else -h) # if need increase, highest room? choose random
  # add tiny to until exact
  for idx in idx:
    step=np.clip(diff,0,1-h[idx]) if diff>0 else np.clip(diff,0,-h[idx])
```
But exact sum floating: We can use `h.sum(dtype=np.float64)`. Need sum(h)*dx=1 equivalently sum n/2 exactly? Floating can't exact but tolerance. Harness likely checks with tolerance? It says sum(h) == n/2 exactly but probably within. Ensure close 1e-12.
Could do final waterfilling exact by selecting element and setting to target - sum(others), if within [0,1]. Since target reachable:
```

  if diff>0:
    j=np.argmax(1-h)
    add=min(diff,1-h[j]); h[j]+=add; diff-=add; ...
```

`symmetrize_feasible(h)`:

```
n=len(h)
m=n//2
left=h[:m]
right=h[n-1-m:n][::-1]
a=(left+right)/2
if n even: h2=np.concatenate([a, a[::-1]])
else: center=(h[m]+h[m])/2? build.
return project_feasible(h2)
```

- If no time return.

But if mu too large, objective dominated by sum; L-BFGS may not move. Projection final partly. Since we call project before/after but during iterations h can drift; penalty keeps. Start small 1e-2. Use 1.0 for high alpha maybe. Previous used 5000 high but perhaps too high. We'll use [1e-2,0.1,1.0,10.0].
  - Run minimize with maxiter scaled by n: maxiter = max(30, min(500, int(150000/n)))? For n=300 500; n=600 250. But L-BFGS maxfun maybe. Use `maxiter=100` per alpha for n<=600. To avoid long, time via options maxiter not time. Could exceed. Use smaller.
- After each, project, evaluate, update. Return best_h, best_c.

Objective functions for L-BFGS need top-level with args. We can define:
```
def smooth_obj_pen(x, alpha, mu, n):
   c = c5_vec_from_n(x,n) # x len n
   m=c.max(); sh=alpha*(c-m); mx=sh.max();...
   val=m+.../alpha + mu*(x.sum()-n/2)**2
   return val
```
But `c5_vec_from_n` assumes len. Could use len(x).
Gradient:
```
def smooth_grad_pen(x, alpha, mu, n):
   c=c5_vec(x)
   sh=alpha*(c-c.max()); mx=sh.max(); w=np.exp(sh-mx); w/=w.sum()
   g=grad_weights(x, w) # includes dx
   g += 2*mu*(x.sum()-n/2)
   return g
```
`grad_weights(x, w)`: Input w same length 2n-1, sums 1, already not dx? We can incorporate dx. `c=corr*dx`. Grad of c = dx*grad_corr. So if w sum 1, grad = dx * ∑ w grad_corr. We can compute grad_corr loop and multiply dx. Previous `wm = w[m]*dx`. We'll implement:
```
def grad_from_weights(h, w):
  n=len(h); dx=2/n; g=zeros(n); L=2*n-1
  for m in range(L):
    if w[m]<=1e-13: continue
    wm = w[m]*dx
    k=m-(n-1)
    if k>=0:
      nv=n-k
      g[:nv] += wm*(1-h[k:])
      g[k:] += wm*(-h[:nv])
    else:
      kk=-k; nv=n-kk
      g[kk:] += wm*(1-h[:nv])
      g[:nv] += wm*(-h[kk:])
  return g
```

`grad_active(h, indices)`: Maybe for active weight solve. We can compute gradient for a single index m by calling with one-hot w. To avoid O(n^2) for K active? O(K n^2) if each full loop over all lags? Actually single-gradient loop can be O(n) for a given lag? Yes for each index m, we can compute gradient in O(n - |k|) not loop all lags. Let's implement `grad_lag(h, m)`:
```
k = m-(n-1)
l=abs(k); nv=n-l
g=zeros(n)
if k>=0:
  l=k; g[l:n] += 1-h[:nv]; g[:nv] -= h[l:n]
else:
  l=-k; g[:nv] += 1-h[l:n]; g[l:n] -= h[:nv]
return g * dx
```
This is O(n). Good. For active K, build G matrix by calling K times, O(K n). Nice. `grad_from_weights` for smooth max loops over all nonzero weights O(L * n) if dense; but weights sparse if alpha high? Could still be all L due exp tails. We can threshold weights: set w[w<1e-10]=0, normalize. This focuses. Good.

`simplex_least_squares(A)`:
Input A shape (n,K). We want w simplex minimize ||A w||. Use multiplicative weights:
```
K=A.shape[1]; w=np.ones(K)/K
C = A.T @ A  # KxK
for it in range(200):
  grad = 2*C @ w
  # projected gradient onto simplex: subtract mean of grad? Since objective over simplex: t = mean(grad); w *= exp(-eta*(grad - t))? This is mirror descent on simplex for convex f.
  t = grad.mean()
  eta = 1.0/(np.max(np.abs(grad - t))+1e-12)
  w *= np.exp(-eta*(grad - t))
  sumw=w.sum(); if tiny break; w/=sumw
  if max change small break
# Ensure nonneg.
```
This converges to optimum for convex quadratic. If K large, okay. If gradient zero, any. Need if A has constant directions? We subtracted column means, so A has rows sum over variables? The objective may have minimum zero if convex combination balanced. Good.
Could also use `scipy.optimize.nnls` with augmented matrix for more accuracy:
```

M=np.vstack([A, beta*np.ones((1,K))]) # for n>K; beta should comparable to ||A w||
b=np.concatenate([np.zeros(A.shape[0]), beta])
# minimize ||M w - b||^2 with w≥0? But this encourages sum=1 and norms=0. Need w not forced positive? nnls gives nonneg. For equivalence, M = [A; beta*1] and b=[0; beta]. If A null, w solves beta(sumw-1)=0. If A not null, approximates. But if A norm scales large, beta adjust. Simpler mirror.
```
Use mirror.

```
def random_pair_search(h, deadline, max_trials, seed, use_equalize=True):
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  n=len(h); best=compute_c5(h)
  rates=[0.05,0.1,0.2,0.45,0.9]
  while time.time()<deadline:
    for _ in range(batch):
      i,j = rng.randint(0,n,2); if i==j continue
      # choose direction: move from higher to lower (equalize) or opposite with prob
      a=h[i]; b=h[j]
      if use_equalize:
        if a>=b: src=i;dst=j; diff=a-b
        else: src=j;dst=i; diff=b-a
      else: random direction
      if diff<1e-8: continue
      for rate in rates:
         d=rate*diff
         # feasibility: if src high to low, after src-d>=0, dst+d<=1 automatically? Since diff<=? If a>=b, a-b<=1; d<=diff; a-d>=b>=0, b+d<=a<=1. good.
         h[src]-=d; h[dst]+=d; c=compute_c5(h); if c<best-1e-13: best=c; improved=True; break
         revert
    if batch ==0 or total time break
  return h,best
```
For move from lower to higher (anti-equalize), use separate option maybe small prob. But equalizing tends to reduce all conflict sum; may eventually flatten h and increase t0; exact will stop.
Also try pair moves not exactly equalizing: rates maybe up to full diff; if full diff accepted, values equal. This could push to local min where adjacent? We'll include rates [0.1,0.25,0.5,0.99]. Also try opposite direction with rate*diff from lower to higher with lower probability? Could help shape. But if no improvement, skip.

- Try rates [0.25,0.5,0.99] for each. Evaluate exact.
- If improved, update best, maybe recompute active after some improvements (e.g., every 20).
- If no improved, break or reduce P.

```
def active_pair_search(h, deadline, topK=8, pairs_per_lag=40, rates=(0.25,0.5,0.75,0.98), max_improvements=None, seed=0):
 h=project; n=len; best=compute; rng maybe not.
 while time<deadline:
   c=compute_c5_vec(h)
   C=best? c.max()
   if C>best: best=C; h=project? no
   idx=np.argsort(c)[-topK:]
   # sort descending c
   idx=idx[np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1]]
   improved_total=False
   for m in idx:
      if time>=deadline: break
      k=m-(n-1); l=abs(k); nv=n-l
      # collect gaps:
      gaps = np.abs(h[:nv] - h[l:n])? If k>=0, pair (i, i+k); if k<0, pair (l+i,i). The gap absolute. We can build arrays vals1, vals2 based on orientation.
      if l==0? lag 0 pairs same index; moving pair invalid. For lag0, active_pair equalization not meaningful (i=j). Skip if l==0.
      # top pairs by gap:
      if nv>0:
        idx_pairs = np.argsort(gap)[::-1][:pairs_per_lag]
        for p in idx_pairs:
          a = h[l+p] if k>=0 else h[p]; b = h[p] if k>=0 else h[l+p]
          # if a>b src=p? Let's define high_val, low_val positions
          if a >= b: src = idx_a; dst=idx_b; diff=a-b
          else: src=idx_b; dst=idx_a; diff=b-a
          if diff < 1e-6: continue
          for rate in rates:
             d=rate*diff
             h[src]-=d; h[dst]+=d
             cnew=compute_c5(h)
             if cnew < best - 1e-12:
                best=cnew; improved_total=True; break
             h[src]+=d; h[dst]-=d
          if improved_total and nv large? continue
   if not improved_total: break
   # optionally project
 return project, best
```

```
    old_n=len(h)
    if old_n==n: return h.copy()
    x_old=(np.arange(old_n)+0.5)*2.0/old_n
    x_new=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
    # np.interp requires increasing x_old; old midpoint. For endpoints, handle.
    return project_feasible(np.interp(x_new, x_old, h, left=h[0], right=h[-1]))
```

`project_feasible` exact:
```
def project_feasible(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    n=len(h); target=0.5*n
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(100):
        s=float(np.sum(h, dtype=np.float64))
        diff=target-s
        if abs(diff)<1e-14:
            break
        if diff>0:
            room=1.0-h
            tot=float(np.sum(room, dtype=np.float64))
            if tot<1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff/tot)*room
        else:
            tot=s
            if tot<1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff/tot)*h
        h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    # fine adjust
    s=float(np.sum(h, dtype=np.float64))
    diff=target-s
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
        # adjust a feasible element
        if diff>0:
            idxs=np.argsort(1.0-h)
        else:
            idxs=np.argsort(h)
        for idx in idxs:
            if diff>0:
                add=min(diff, 1.0-h[idx])
            else:
                add=max(diff, -h[idx])
            if add==0: continue
            h[idx]+=add; diff-=add
            if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
    return h
```
If after adjustments sum not exactly due floating, enough.

`symmetrize_feasible`:
```
def symmetrize_feasible(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    n=len(h); m=n//2
    left=h[:m]
    right=h[n-1-m:n][::-1]
    a=0.5*(left+right)
    if n%2==0:
       h2=np.concatenate([a, a[::-1]])
    else:
       h2=np.concatenate([a, [0.5*(h[m]+h[m])], a[::-1]]) # center? actually for odd, middle pair? a has m, center index m.
    return project_feasible(h2)
```
For odd, left length m, right length m, center h[m]. sym_full = [a, center, a[::-1]]. Good.

`lbfgs functions`:
```
def _lbfgs_obj(x, alpha, mu, n):
    c=c5_vec(x)
    m=c.max()
    z=alpha*(c-m)
    zm=z.max()
    if zm>500:
       lse=zm+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z-zm)))
    else:
       lse=np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z)))
    val=m + lse/alpha
    diff=x.sum()-float(n)/2.0
    val += mu*diff*diff
    return float(val)

def _lbfgs_grad(x, alpha, mu, n):
    c=c5_vec(x)
    m=c.max()
    z=alpha*(c-m)
    zm=z.max()
    if zm>500:
       w=np.exp(z-zm)
    else:
       w=np.exp(z)
    s=w.sum()
    if s<=0: w=np.full_like(c,1.0/len(c))
    else: w=w/s
    # threshold and renormalize
    w[w<1e-12]=0
    sw=w.sum()
    if sw>0: w/=sw
    else: w=np.full_like(c,1.0/len(c))
    g=grad_weights(x,w)
    g += 2.0*mu*(x.sum()-float(n)/2.0)
    return g
```
But `_lbfgs_obj` and `_lbfgs_grad` recompute c; L-BFGS alternates but scipy may call obj and grad at same x; no cache. Could implement caching? Not needed.

`grad_weights(h,w)` must use len(h); we can multiply dx inside.
```
def grad_weights(h,w):
 n=len(h); L=2*n-1; dx=2.0/n; g=np.zeros(n)
 if len(w)!=L: ...
 for m in range(L):
   wm=w[m]
   if wm==0: continue
   wm *= dx
   k=m-(n-1)
   if k>=0:
     l=k; nv=n-l
     if nv<=0: continue
     g[l:n] += wm*(1.0-h[:nv])
     g[:nv] -= wm*h[l:n]
   else:
     l=-k; nv=n-l
     if nv<=0: continue
     g[:nv] += wm*(1.0-h[l:n])
     g[l:n] -= wm*h[:nv]
 return g
```
Verify signs with k>=0: earlier g[l:] += 1-h[:nv]; g[:nv] -= h[l:]. This code uses g[:nv] -= wm*h[l:n] yes; g[l:n] += wm*(1-h[:nv]). Good. k<0: g[:nv]+=1-h[l:n]; g[l:n]-=h[:nv]. Good.

`grad_lag(h,m)`:
```
def grad_lag(h,m):
 n=len(h); dx=2/n; l_off=m-(n-1); l=abs(l_off); nv=n-l; g=zeros
 if nv<=0: return g
 if l_off>=0:
   g[l:n] += dx*(1-h[:nv])
   g[:nv] -= dx*h[l:n]
 else:
   g[:nv] += dx*(1-h[l:n])
   g[l:n] -= dx*h[:nv]
 return g
```

`lbfgs_optimize`:

```
def lbfgs_optimize(h, stop_time, alphas, mus, maxiter=None):
 h=project_feasible(h); n=len(h); bounds=[(0,1)]*n
 if maxiter is None:
    maxiter=120 if n<=400 else 70
 best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h)
 # sort alphas? We pass list increasing.
 nalpha=len(alphas)
 for ai, alpha in enumerate(alphas):
   remaining=stop_time - time.time()
   if remaining < 8: break
   # reserve for later? if ai < nalpha-1 and remaining < 5*(nalpha-ai): break? But maybe still run one.
   mu = mus[ai % len(mus)]
   # maybe if alpha very high, fewer maxiter? no.
   try:
     res=minimize(_lbfgs_obj, h, jac=_lbfgs_grad, args=(alpha, mu, n), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol':1e-16, 'gtol':1e-11, 'maxfun':maxiter*8, 'maxls':30, 'disp':False})
     h2=project_feasible(res.x)
     c2=compute_c5(h2)
     if c2 < best_c - 1e-15:
        best_c=c2; best_h=h2.copy()
     elif res.x is not None:
        # still maybe res.x slightly better? Use h2 not accepted? If not better, maybe keep old to not worsen. But L-BFGS with smooth max may reduce smooth not exact; accept only exact.
        pass
   except Exception:
     pass
   h=best_h.copy() # continue from best exact
   if time.time() > stop_time - 5: break
 return best_h,best_c
```
Potential: L-BFGS may not improve exact for low alpha due local; continuing from old best each alpha okay. But if L-BFGS lands in different basin? It starts from best; if exact doesn't improve, we revert. Good. Could miss improvements if res.x slightly worse exact but better smooth/high alpha? Not important.

`simplex_mirror(w, C)`? Top-level.
```
def simplex_least_squares(A):
    K=A.shape[1]
    if K==0: return None
    if K==1: return np.array([1.0])
    w=np.full(K,1.0/K)
    C=A.T.dot(A)
    # diagonal positive
    for it in range(100):
       grad=2.0*(C.dot(w))
       t=grad.mean()
       d=grad-t
       em=np.max(np.abs(d))
       if em<1e-12: break
       eta=0.5/em # step size
       w *= np.exp(-eta*d)
       s=w.sum()
       if s<=1e-300: w=np.full(K,1.0/K); break
       w/=s
       if np.max(np.abs(w - (1.0/K)))<1e-8? maybe break? not necessary
    return w
```
But objective minimize ||A w||^2, gradient 2A^T A w. Mirror updates with gradient relative to mean. Good.
However if A has no solution zero, weights may put all mass on smallest column; that's fine. The direction g = A^T w (before centering? G columns are active gradients, A=Gc? We used A=Gc (n x K), and w minimize ||Gc w||. Then gradient to use = G^T w - mean(G^T w). Since Gc = G - means, Gc^T w = G^T w - sum w mean_j = G^T w - mean(G^T w)? Wait mean of each column not same; G^T w components: ∑w_j G_{ij}; minus mean over i = ∑w_j(G_{ij} - mean_i G_{ij}) = Gc^T w. So centered. Good.

`active_weight_descent(h, stop_time, K=12, max_steps=200)`:
```
h=project; best=compute; 
for step in range(max_steps):
 if time>=stop_time: break
 c=c5_vec(h); C=c.max(); best = min(best,C)
 # active: include idx with C - c < max(1e-5, 0.05*C?) or topK.
 tol=max(1e-5, 0.01*(C- (c.min()? no)))? Simpler topK + those within 1e-4.
 idx=np.where(c >= C - max(1e-4, 0.02*C))[0]
 if len(idx)<3: idx=np.argsort(c)[-K:]
 elif len(idx)>K: idx=np.argsort(c)[-K:]
 if len(idx)==0: break
 G=np.array([grad_lag(h,int(m)) for m in idx])
 A=G - G.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
 # if norm small? compute
 w=simplex_least_squares(A)
 g=G.T.dot(w)
 g -= g.mean()
 ng=np.linalg.norm(g)
 if ng<1e-10: break
 d=-g
 # scale
 maxd=np.max(np.abs(d))
 if maxd<1e-15: break
 d *= 0.05/maxd
 steps=[1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005]
 improved=False
 for s in steps:
   h_try=project_feasible(h + s*d)
   c_try=compute_c5(h_try)
   if c_try < best - 1e-13:
      h=h_try; best=c_try; improved=True; break
 if not improved:
   # try uniform active weights as fallback? already maybe. break if no
   # maybe step larger? d already 0.05; maybe h bounds. Skip.
   break
return h,best
```

```
def consider_solution(state, h, c):
    try:
        c=float(c)
    except: return
    if h is None: return
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    if h.size == 0: return
    if np.any(h < -1e-12) or np.any(h > 1+1e-12): h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    if c < state[0] - 1e-12:
        state[0]=c
        state[1]=h.copy()
        state[2]=len(h)
```

Now, active_pair_search time: It computes c vector once per pass and then many full objective evaluations. In code, `best = compute_c5(h)` initially; inside after each improvement update. Implement `active_pair_search` with `max_passes` maybe 1000 but time stops.
```
def active_pair_search(h, deadline, topK=8, pairs_per_lag=40, rates=(0.25,0.5,0.85), seed=0):
    h=project_feasible(h); n=len(h); best=compute_c5(h)
    if n<2 or topK<1: return h,best
    while time.time() < deadline:
        c_vec=c5_vec(h); best=max(best,float(c_vec.max()))? if h changed? c_vec max should <=best? Actually best is objective; use best=c=max for consistency.
        C=float(c_vec.max())
        # if C > best: best=C
        idx_all=np.argsort(c_vec)[::-1][:topK]
        # Also include near max? Already top.
        improved=False
        for m in idx_all:
            if time.time() >= deadline: break
            off=int(m-(n-1)); l=abs(off); nv=n-l
            if l==0 or nv<=0: continue
            gap=np.abs(h[:nv]-h[l:n])
            if gap.max() < 1e-8: continue
            # top pairs
            if pairs_per_lag < nv:
                pair_idx=np.argsort(gap)[::-1][:pairs_per_lag]
            else: pair_idx=np.arange(nv)
            for p in pair_idx:
                if time.time() >= deadline: break
                p=int(p); a=float(h[p]); b=float(h[p+l])
                if a>=b:
                    src=p; dst=p+l; diff=a-b
                else:
                    src=p+l; dst=p; diff=b-a
                if diff < 1e-7: continue
                # maxd=min(diff, h[src], 1-h[dst]) but diff selected; use min(diff, h[src],1-h[dst])
                maxd=min(diff, float(h[src]), 1.0-float(h[dst]))
                if maxd <= 0: continue
                for rate in rates:
                    d=rate*maxd
                    if d <= 1e-15: continue
                    h[src]-=d; h[dst]+=d
                    cnew=compute_c5(h)
                    if cnew < best - 1e-13:
                        best=cnew; improved=True
                        break
                    h[src]+=d; h[dst]-=d
            if improved:
                # if many improvements, break out of idx loop to recompute active
                if time.time() < deadline: continue
                else break
        if not improved:
            break
        # Optional project every loop? Pair moves preserve; but do every 10?
    h=project_feasible(h); return h,best
```
One issue: Using `best` from previous; after active pair improvements, best lower. At start of while, C could be >best due floating? Set `best = float(c_vec.max())` (since h projected and consistent) instead of max with old. Use `best = float(c_vec.max())` to avoid stale. Before while, compute c_vec.
```
while time...:
  c_vec=c5_vec(h); C=float(c_vec.max())
  if C < best: best=C
  ...
```
After no improvement break. If improved, loop recomputes.

If many tiny improvements, could loop many; each pass has at least one improvement. time limits.

`random_pair_search`:
```
def random_pair_search(h, deadline, max_trials=10000, seed=1, anti_prob=0.2):
    h=project_feasible(h); n=len; best=compute; rng=RandomState(seed)
    rates=np.array([0.08,0.16,0.28,0.45,0.65,0.85,1.0])
    no_improve=0
    trials=0
    while trials < max_trials and time.time() < deadline:
       trials+=1
       i,j=rng.randint(0,n,2)
       if i==j: no_improve+=1; continue
       a=float(h[i]); b=float(h[j])
       if a > b:
          hi=i; lo=j
       else: hi=j; lo=i
       if rng.rand() >= anti_prob:
           src=hi; dst=lo; maxd=min(diff, h[src],1-h[dst]) # diff=abs
       else:
           src=lo; dst=hi; maxd=min(h[src],1-h[dst])
       if maxd <= 0: no_improve+=1; continue
       r=float(rng.choice(rates))
       d=r*maxd
       if d<=1e-15: no_improve+=1; continue
       h[src]-=d; h[dst]+=d
       cnew=compute_c5(h)
       if cnew < best - 1e-14:
          best=cnew; no_improve=0
       else:
          h[src]+=d; h[dst]-=d; no_improve+=1
       if no_improve > 5000:
          # reduce maxd? Or break? Could break to avoid waste.
          if no_improve > max(2000, max_trials//2): break
    h=project_feasible(h); return h,best
```
If anti, maxd may be small; rates full might make low to 0 or high to1; good. If no improve after many, break. But maybe need continue with smaller rates; rates already random small. Break after 5000 may be too early. Use `if no_improve > 8000: break`. Or keep until trials. Time limit enough.

Maybe we should choose pair based on gradient to guide. But random okay.

Now, active_weight_descent: Let's ensure it doesn't infinite. Use `max_steps=100`, stop_time.
```
def active_weight_descent(h, deadline, K=12, active_tol=1e-4, max_steps=100):
  h=project; n=len; best=compute
  for _ in range(max_steps):
    if time.time()>=deadline: break
    c=c5_vec(h); C=float(c.max()); best=min(best,C)
    # active idx: topK plus tolerance
    close_idx = np.where(c >= C - max(active_tol, 0.01*C))[0]
    if len(close_idx) < 3:
       idx = np.argsort(c)[-K:]
    elif len(close_idx) > K:
       idx = close_idx[np.argsort(c[close_idx])[-K:]]? This uses no lambda.
       scores=c[close_idx]; ordered=np.argsort(scores)[::-1]; idx=close_idx[ordered[:K]]
    else: idx=close_idx
    if len(idx)==0 or int(idx[0])? maybe all lag0; still okay.
    G=[]
    for m in idx: G.append(grad_lag(h,int(m)))
    G=np.vstack(G)
    means=G.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    A=G - means
    w=simplex_least_squares(A)
    if w is None: break
    g=G.T.dot(w)
    g -= g.mean()
    ng=np.linalg.norm(g)
    if ng<1e-12: break
    d=-g; maxd=np.max(np.abs(d)); if maxd<=0: break
    d *= 0.05/maxd
    improved=False
    for s in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005):
       if time.time()>=deadline: break
       h_try=project_feasible(h + s*d)
       ctry=compute_c5(h_try)
       if ctry < best - 1e-13:
          h=h_try; best=ctry; improved=True; break
    if not improved:
       # try larger step? d scaled 0.05 max; if bounds, no. Break.
       break
  h=project_feasible(h); return h,best
```
`simplex_least_squares` could fail if A all zeros; returns uniform. If w uniform and g zero, break. Good.

Potential bug: `grad_lag` for m indices where l >= n? m range 0..2n-2, l<=n-1, nv>=1. okay.

`project_feasible` final fine adjustment:
```
    s = float(np.sum(h, dtype=np.float64))
    diff = target - s
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
        order = np.argsort(1.0 - h) if diff > 0 else np.argsort(h)
        for idx in order:
            if diff > 0:
                add = min(diff, 1.0 - float(h[idx]))
            else:
                add = max(diff, -float(h[idx]))
            if add == 0.0: continue
            h[idx] += add
            diff -= add
            if abs(diff) < 1e-15: break
```
If diff large due initial infeasible (target maybe > sum), the proportional loop should handle. Final order may not fully adjust if one element not enough? But target <=n and after projection, diff small. If not, loop over all.

`make_cosine(n,A)`:
```
def make_cosine(n, A):
    n=int(max(2,n))
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
    r=np.abs(x-1.0)
    cos=np.cos(np.pi*r/2.0)
    A=float(A)
    B=(n/2.0 - A*cos.sum())/n
    h=A*cos + B
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    return project_feasible(h)
```
If B<0, clip and project. Good.

`interp_to`:
```
def interp_to(h, n):
    old_n=len(h); n=int(max(2,n))
    if old_n == n: return project_feasible(h.copy())
    x_old=(np.arange(old_n)+0.5)*2.0/old_n
    x_new=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
    y=np.interp(x_new, x_old, h, left=float(h[0]), right=float(h[-1]))
    return project_feasible(y)
```
`np.interp` requires x_old increasing; yes.

`simplex_least_squares`: If A shape (n,K). If K=0 return None. If K=1 return [1]. Use mirror. Need if A has NaN? not.
```
def simplex_least_squares(A):
    n,K=A.shape
    if K<=0: return None
    if K==1: return np.array([1.0])
    w=np.full(K,1.0/K)
    C=A.T.dot(A)
    for _ in range(80):
       grad=2.0*C.dot(w)
       centered=grad - grad.mean()
       emax=np.max(np.abs(centered))
       if emax < 1e-12: break
       eta=1.0/(emax+1e-12)
       # scale eta maybe 0.5 to stabilize
       w = w * np.exp(-0.5*eta*centered)
       s=w.sum()
       if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
          w=np.full(K,1.0/K); break
       w/=s
       # if all mass one, maybe keep
    return w
```
This minimizes ||Aw||^2? The mirror descent step uses gradient centered to preserve sum. The objective over simplex; gradient for update is grad, Lagrange multiplier t=mean(grad). Centered correct. Good.

`active_weight_descent`: After `G=np.vstack([grad_lag(...)])`; if K large, memory okay. `Gc = G - G.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)`. If `np.linalg.norm(Gc)` small, break. w = simplex_least_squares(Gc). `g = G.T.dot(w)`. `g -= g.mean()`. If norm small, break.
`d = -g`; scale. If `project_feasible` changes sum significantly? d sum maybe zero but projection may alter. Fine.
Step list. If no improvement, maybe try passive: if active all q0 (lag0) gradient may have zero mean? For q0 gradient =1-2h_i; mean=1-2*S/n =1-1=0, direction -g = - (1-2h)=2h-1, moves high up low down, polarizes. Good.

Let's write run with explicit phases.

Pseudo:
```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    try: total=float(budget_s)
    except: total=1100.0
    if total < 10: total=10.0
    deadline=t0+max(1.0,total-5.0)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    state=[inf,None,0]
    # init candidates
    try: ... parent
    ...
    if state[1] is None: state fallback
    # define segments based on total rest
    rest=deadline-t0
    if rest < 30:
       h=project_feasible(state[1]); c=compute; return h,c,len(h)
    seg1_stop=t0+0.33*rest
    seg2_stop=t0+0.68*rest
    seg3_stop=deadline
    # Choose alt n
    if total >= 950: n_alt=600
    elif total >= 550: n_alt=450
    else: n_alt=state[2]
    # shape_alt init
    shape_alt=None; shape_alt_c=inf
    if n_alt != state[2] and time.time() < deadline - 250:
       for A in [0.60,0.66,0.70,0.74]:
          h=make_cosine(n_alt,A); c=compute_c5(h)
          if c < state[0] + 0.025 and c < shape_alt_c:
             shape_alt=h.copy(); shape_alt_c=c
          if c < state[0] + 0.025:
             consider_solution(state,h,c)
    # But consider_solution projects and recomputes; shape_alt should be projected. okay.
    # seg1 main
    h=state[1].copy()
    n_h=len(h)
    maxit_main = 90 if n_h>600 else (120 if n_h<=350 else 80)
    if time.time() < seg1_stop - 15:
       stop=min(seg1_stop-2.0, time.time()+100.0)
       h,c=lbfgs_optimize(h, stop, [20.0,200.0,2000.0], [1e-3,1e-2,0.1], maxiter=maxit_main)
       consider_solution(state,h,c)
       h=state[1].copy()
    if time.time() < seg1_stop - 30:
       stop=min(seg1_stop-20.0, time.time()+45.0)
       h,c=active_weight_descent(h, stop, K=12, max_steps=80)
       consider_solution(state,h,c)
       h=state[1].copy()
    if time.time() < seg1_stop - 40:
       stop=min(seg1_stop-10.0, time.time()+80.0)
       topK=8 if len(h)<=400 else 6
       ppl=50 if len(h)<=400 else 30
       h,c=active_pair_search(h, stop, topK=topK, pairs_per_lag=ppl, rates=(0.25,0.5,0.85), seed=seed+11)
       consider_solution(state,h,c)
       h=state[1].copy()
    if time.time() < seg1_stop - 5:
       stop=min(seg1_stop-2.0, time.time()+70.0)
       h,c=lbfgs_optimize(h, stop, [20000.0,200000.0,1000000.0], [1.0,10.0,20.0], maxiter=maxit_main)
       consider_solution(state,h,c)
       h=state[1].copy()
    if time.time() < seg1_stop - 2:
       stop=min(seg1_stop-1.0, time.time()+40.0)
       tr=15000 if len(h)<=400 else 5000
       h,c=random_pair_search(h, stop, max_trials=tr, seed=seed+21, anti_prob=0.25)
       consider_solution(state,h,c)
       h=state[1].copy()

    # seg2 second candidate
    if time.time() < seg2_stop - 20:
        base=state[1].copy()
        base_n=len(base)
        if n_alt != base_n:
           h_int=interp_to(base,n_alt)
        else:
           h_int=base.copy()
        c_int=compute_c5(h_int)
        h_sym=symmetrize_feasible(h_int); c_sym=compute_c5(h_sym)
        # choose second
        if c_int <= c_sym:
           second_h=h_int; second_c=c_int
        else:
           second_h=h_sym; second_c=c_sym
        # if shape_alt not None and within 0.015 of best second, use shape for diversity
        if shape_alt is not None:
           if shape_alt_c <= second_c + 0.005:
              second_h=shape_alt.copy(); second_c=shape_alt_c
           # also consider shape
        # If second_n == state[2] and c close and likely same, maybe still optimize? Could skip to save? We'll optimize if n_alt != state[2] or second_c < state[0] + 0.002.

            pass

            if time.time() < seg2_stop - 20:

               h2,c2=active_pair_search(h2, stop, topK=topK, pairs_per_lag=ppl, rates=(0.25,0.5,0.85), seed=seed+31)
               consider_solution(state,h2,c2); h2=state[1].copy()
            if time.time() < seg2_stop - 10:

            if time.time() < seg2_stop - 2:

               consider_solution(state,h2,c2); h2=state[1].copy()
```

```python
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def c5_vec(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * (2.0 / n)


def c5_value(h):
    c = c5_vec(h)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.max(c))


def project_feasible(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    n = len(h)
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(200):
        s = float(np.sum(h, dtype=np.float64))
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(np.sum(room, dtype=np.float64))
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = s
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * h
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    s = float(np.sum(h, dtype=np.float64))
    diff = target - s
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
        if diff > 0.0:
            order = np.argsort(1.0 - h)
        else:
            order = np.argsort(h)
        for idx in order:
            if diff > 0.0:
                add = min(diff, 1.0 - float(h[idx]))
            else:
                add = max(diff, -float(h[idx]))
            if add == 0.0:
                continue
            h[idx] += add
            diff -= add
            if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
                break

    return h


def symmetrize_feasible(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    n = len(h)
    m = n // 2
    left = h[:m]
    right = h[n - 1 - m:n][::-1]
    half = 0.5 * (left + right)
    if n % 2 == 0:
        out = np.concatenate([half, half[::-1]])
    else:
        out = np.concatenate([half, np.array([0.5 * (h[m] + h[m])], dtype=float), half[::-1]])
    return project_feasible(out)


def interp_to(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    old_n = len(h)
    n = int(max(2, n))
    if old_n == n:
        return project_feasible(h)
    x_old = (np.arange(old_n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / old_n)
    x_new = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h, left=float(h[0]), right=float(h[-1]))
    return project_feasible(y)


def make_triangle(n):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    h = 1.0 - np.abs(x - 1.0)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return project_feasible(h)


def make_cosine(n, A):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    r = np.abs(x - 1.0)
    cosv = np.cos(0.5 * np.pi * r)
    A = float(A)
    B = (0.5 * n - A * float(np.sum(cosv))) / float(n)
    h = A * cosv + B
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return project_feasible(h)


def random_candidate(n, rng):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    h = 0.5 * np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    freqs = [0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0]
    for f in freqs:
        amp = rng.uniform(0.0, 0.22)
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        h += amp * np.cos(np.pi * f * x + ph)
    h += rng.normal(0.0, 0.008, n)
    return project_feasible(h)


def grad_weights(h, w):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=float)
    if len(w) != L:
        w = np.interp(np.arange(L), np.arange(len(w)), w)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    for m in range(L):
        wm = float(w[m])
        if wm <= 1e-13:
            continue
        wm *= dx
        k = m - (n - 1)
        if k >= 0:
            l = k
            nv = n - l
            if nv <= 0:
                continue
            g[l:n] += wm * (1.0 - h[:nv])
            g[:nv] -= wm * h[l:n]
        else:
            l = -k
            nv = n - l
            if nv <= 0:
                continue
            g[:nv] += wm * (1.0 - h[l:n])
            g[l:n] -= wm * h[:nv]
    return g


def grad_lag(h, m):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    k = int(m) - (n - 1)
    l = abs(k)
    nv = n - l
    if nv <= 0:
        return g
    if k >= 0:
        g[l:n] += dx * (1.0 - h[:nv])
        g[:nv] -= dx * h[l:n]
    else:
        g[:nv] += dx * (1.0 - h[l:n])
        g[l:n] -= dx * h[:nv]
    return g


def smooth_max_value(h, alpha):
    alpha = float(max(1.0, alpha))
    c = c5_vec(h)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    z = alpha * (c - m)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return m
    return float(m + np.log(s) / alpha)


def smooth_gradient(h, alpha):
    alpha = float(max(1.0, alpha))
    c = c5_vec(h)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    z = alpha * (c - m)
    w = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.full_like(w, 1.0 / len(w))
    else:
        w /= s
    w[w < 1e-12] = 0.0
    sw = float(np.sum(w))
    if sw <= 0.0:
        w = np.full_like(w, 1.0 / len(w))
    else:
        w /= sw
    return grad_weights(h, w)


def _lbfgs_obj(x, alpha, mu, n):
    val = smooth_max_value(x, alpha)
    diff = float(np.sum(x, dtype=np.float64)) - 0.5 * float(n)
    val += float(mu) * diff * diff
    return float(val)


def _lbfgs_grad(x, alpha, mu, n):
    g = smooth_gradient(x, alpha)
    diff = float(np.sum(x, dtype=np.float64)) - 0.5 * float(n)
    g += 2.0 * float(mu) * diff
    return g


def lbfgs_optimize(h, stop_time, alphas, mus, maxiter=None):
    h = project_feasible(h)
    n = len(h)
    if maxiter is None:
        if n <= 350:
            maxiter = 120
        elif n <= 600:
            maxiter = 70
        else:
            maxiter = 45
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5_value(h)

    for idx, alpha in enumerate(alphas):
        if time.time() > float(stop_time) - 5.0:
            break
        mu = float(mus[idx % len(mus)])
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _lbfgs_obj,
                h,
                jac=_lbfgs_grad,
                args=(alpha, mu, n),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                    "maxfun": int(maxiter * 8),
                    "ftol": 1e-16,
                    "gtol": 1e-10,
                    "maxls": 30,
                    "disp": False,
                },
            )
            h2 = project_feasible(res.x)
            c2 = c5_value(h2)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-15:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass
        h = best_h.copy()
        if time.time() > float(stop_time) - 3.0:
            break

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h)
    c_final = c5_value(best_h)
    if c_final < best_c:
        best_c = c_final
    return best_h, float(best_c)


def _slsqp_obj(x, alpha, n):
    return smooth_max_value(x, alpha)


def _slsqp_grad(x, alpha, n):
    return smooth_gradient(x, alpha)


def _eq_sum(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x, dtype=np.float64)) - 0.5 * float(n)


def _eq_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones(len(x), dtype=float)


def slsqp_optimize(h, stop_time, alpha, maxiter=50):
    h = project_feasible(h)
    n = len(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5_value(h)
    if time.time() > float(stop_time) - 30.0:
        return best_h, best_c

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [
        {
            "type": "eq",
            "fun": _eq_sum,
            "jac": _eq_jac,
            "args": (n,),
        }
    ]
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _slsqp_obj,
            h,
            jac=_slsqp_grad,
            args=(alpha, n),
            method="SLSQP",
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "ftol": 1e-15,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
        h2 = project_feasible(res.x)
        c2 = c5_value(h2)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-15:
            best_h = h2.copy()
            best_c = c2
    except Exception:
        pass

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h)
    cf = c5_value(best_h)
    if cf < best_c:
        best_c = cf
    return best_h, float(best_c)


def simplex_least_squares(A):
    A = np.asarray(A, dtype=float)
    if A.size == 0:
        return None
    n, K = A.shape
    if K == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=float)

    w = np.full(K, 1.0 / K, dtype=float)
    C = A.T.dot(A)
    for _ in range(100):
        grad = 2.0 * (C.dot(w))
        centered = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
        em = float(np.max(np.abs(centered)))
        if em < 1e-12:
            break
        eta = 1.0 / (em + 1e-12)
        w = w * np.exp(-0.5 * eta * centered)
        s = float(np.sum(w))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            w = np.full(K, 1.0 / K, dtype=float)
            break
        w /= s
    return w


def active_weight_descent(h, deadline, K=12, max_steps=100):
    h = project_feasible(h)
    n = len(h)
    best = c5_value(h)

    for _ in range(max_steps):
        if time.time() > float(deadline):
            break
        c = c5_vec(h)
        C = float(np.max(c))
        if C < best:
            best = C

        tol = max(1e-4, 0.01 * C)
        close = np.where(c >= C - tol)[0]
        if len(close) < 3:
            idx = np.argsort(c)[-max(1, K):][::-1]
        elif len(close) > K:
            scores = c[close]
            order = np.argsort(scores)[::-1]
            idx = close[order[:K]]
        else:
            idx = close

        if len(idx) == 0:
            break

        Glist = []
        for m in idx:
            Glist.append(grad_lag(h, int(m)))
        G = np.vstack(Glist)
        A = G - np.mean(G, axis=0, keepdims=True)
        w = simplex_least_squares(A)
        if w is None:
            break
        g = G.T.dot(w)
        g -= float(np.mean(g))
        ng = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if ng < 1e-12:
            break

        d = -g
        md = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
        if md < 1e-15:
            break
        d *= 0.05 / md

        improved = False
        for step in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005):
            if time.time() > float(deadline):
                break
            h_try = project_feasible(h + step * d)
            c_try = c5_value(h_try)
            if c_try < best - 1e-13:
                h = h_try
                best = c_try
                improved = True
                break
        if not improved:
            break

    h = project_feasible(h)
    cf = c5_value(h)
    if cf < best:
        best = cf
    return h, float(best)


def active_pair_search(h, deadline, topK=8, pairs_per_lag=45,
                       rates=(0.25, 0.5, 0.85), seed=0):
    h = project_feasible(h)
    n = len(h)
    if n < 2:
        return h, c5_value(h)
    best = c5_value(h)

    while time.time() < float(deadline):
        c = c5_vec(h)
        C = float(np.max(c))
        if C < best:
            best = C

        idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:max(1, int(topK))]
        moves = 0
        improved_total = False

        for m in idx:
            if moves >= 20 or time.time() >= float(deadline):
                break
            off = int(m) - (n - 1)
            l = abs(off)
            nv = n - l
            if l == 0 or nv <= 0:
                continue

            gap = np.abs(h[:nv] - h[l:n])
            if float(np.max(gap)) < 1e-8:
                continue

            if int(pairs_per_lag) < nv:
                pair_idx = np.argsort(gap)[::-1][:int(pairs_per_lag)]
            else:
                pair_idx = np.arange(nv)

            for p in pair_idx:
                if moves >= 20 or time.time() >= float(deadline):
                    break
                p = int(p)
                a = float(h[p])
                b = float(h[p + l])
                if a >= b:
                    src = p
                    dst = p + l
                    diff = a - b
                else:
                    src = p + l
                    dst = p
                    diff = b - a
                if diff < 1e-7:
                    continue
                maxd = min(diff, float(h[src]), 1.0 - float(h[dst]))
                if maxd <= 0.0:
                    continue

                for rate in rates:
                    d = float(rate) * maxd
                    if d <= 1e-15:
                        continue
                    h[src] -= d
                    h[dst] += d
                    cnew = c5_value(h)
                    if cnew < best - 1e-13:
                        best = cnew
                        moves += 1
                        improved_total = True
                        break
                    h[src] += d
                    h[dst] -= d

        if not improved_total:
            break

    h = project_feasible(h)
    cf = c5_value(h)
    if cf < best:
        best = cf
    return h, float(best)


def random_pair_search(h, deadline, max_trials=10000, seed=1, anti_prob=0.2):
    h = project_feasible(h)
    n = len(h)
    if n < 2:
        return h, c5_value(h)

    best = c5_value(h)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    rates = np.array([0.08, 0.16, 0.28, 0.45, 0.65, 0.85, 1.0], dtype=float)
    no_improve = 0

    for _ in range(int(max_trials)):
        if time.time() >= float(deadline):
            break

        i, j = rng.randint(0, n, 2)
        if i == j:
            no_improve += 1
            continue

        a = float(h[i])
        b = float(h[j])
        if a > b:
            hi, lo = i, j
        else:
            hi, lo = j, i

        anti = rng.rand() < float(anti_prob)
        if not anti:
            src, dst = hi, lo
            maxd = min(abs(a - b), float(h[src]), 1.0 - float(h[dst]))
        else:
            src, dst = lo, hi
            maxd = min(float(h[src]), 1.0 - float(h[dst]))

        if maxd <= 0.0:
            no_improve += 1
            continue

        d = float(rng.choice(rates)) * maxd
        if d <= 1e-15:
            no_improve += 1
            continue

        h[src] -= d
        h[dst] += d
        cnew = c5_value(h)
        if cnew < best - 1e-14:
            best = cnew
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            h[src] += d
            h[dst] -= d
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > max(3000, int(max_trials) // 2):
            break

    h = project_feasible(h)
    cf = c5_value(h)
    if cf < best:
        best = cf
    return h, float(best)


def consider_solution(state, h, c):
    if h is None:
        return
    try:
        h = project_feasible(h)
        c = c5_value(h)
    except Exception:
        return
    if h.size > 0 and c < state[0] - 1e-12:
        state[0] = float(c)
        state[1] = h.copy()
        state[2] = len(h)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    try:
        total = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total = 1100.0
    if total < 10.0:
        total = 10.0
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, total - 5.0)

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    state = [float("inf"), None, 0]

    try:
        parent = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        if parent.size < 2:
            raise ValueError
    except Exception:
        parent = make_triangle(300)

    parent = project_feasible(parent)
    consider_solution(state, parent, c5_value(parent))
    consider_solution(state, symmetrize_feasible(parent), c5_value(symmetrize_feasible(parent)))

    if state[1] is None:
        h0 = make_triangle(300)
        c0 = c5_value(h0)
        state = [c0, h0.copy(), len(h0)]

    n_par = state[2]
    consider_solution(state, make_triangle(n_par), c5_value(make_triangle(n_par)))

    for A in (0.50, 0.58, 0.64, 0.70, 0.74, 0.78):
        h = make_cosine(n_par, A)
        c = c5_value(h)
        if c < state[0] + 0.03:
            consider_solution(state, h, c)

    for _ in range(2):
        h = random_candidate(n_par, rng)
        c = c5_value(h)
        if c < state[0] + 0.04:
            consider_solution(state, h, c)

    if total >= 950.0:
        n_alt = 600
    elif total >= 550.0:
        n_alt = 450
    else:
        n_alt = n_par

    shape_alt = None
    shape_alt_c = float("inf")
    if n_alt != n_par and time.time() < deadline - 260.0:
        for A in (0.60, 0.66, 0.70, 0.74):
            h = make_cosine(n_alt, A)
            c = c5_value(h)
            if c < state[0] + 0.025:
                consider_solution(state, h, c)
                if c < shape_alt_c:
                    shape_alt_c = c
                    shape_alt = h.copy()
        for _ in range(2):
            h = random_candidate(n_alt, rng)
            c = c5_value(h)
            if c < state[0] + 0.03:
                consider_solution(state, h, c)
                if c < shape_alt_c:
                    shape_alt_c = c
                    shape_alt = h.copy()

    rest = deadline - t0
    if rest < 30.0:
        h = project_feasible(state[1])
        c = c5_value(h)
        return h, float(c), len(h)

    seg1_stop = t0 + 0.33 * rest
    seg2_stop = t0 + 0.68 * rest
    seg3_stop = deadline

    h = state[1].copy()
    n_h = len(h)
    if n_h <= 350:
        maxit_main = 120
    elif n_h <= 600:
        maxit_main = 75
    else:
        maxit_main = 45

    if time.time() < seg1_stop - 15.0:
        stop = min(seg1_stop - 2.0, time.time() + 100.0)
        h, c = lbfgs_optimize(h, stop, [20.0, 200.0, 2000.0],
                              [1e-3, 1e-2, 0.1], maxiter=maxit_main)
        consider_solution(state, h, c)
        h = state[1].copy()

    if time.time() < seg1_stop - 30.0:
        stop = min(seg1_stop - 20.0, time.time() + 45.0)
        h, c = active_weight_descent(h, stop, K=12, max_steps=80)
        consider_solution(state, h, c)
        h = state[1].copy()

    if time.time() < seg1_stop - 40.0:
        stop = min(seg1_stop - 12.0, time.time() + 80.0)
        topK = 8 if len(h) <= 400 else 6
        ppl = 50 if len(h) <= 400 else 30
        h, c = active_pair_search(h, stop, topK=topK, pairs_per_lag=ppl,
                                  rates=(0.25, 0.5, 0.85), seed=seed + 11)
        consider_solution(state, h, c)
        h = state[1].copy()

    if time.time() < seg1_stop - 10.0:
        stop = min(seg1_stop - 4.0, time.time() + 70.0)
        h, c = lbfgs_optimize(h, stop, [20000.0, 200000.0, 1000000.0],
                              [1.0, 10.0, 20.0], maxiter=maxit_main)
        consider_solution(state, h, c)
        h = state[1].copy()

    if time.time() < seg1_stop - 2.0:
        stop = min(seg1_stop - 1.0, time.time() + 40.0)
        trials = 18000 if len(h) <= 400 else 6000
        h, c = random_pair_search(h, stop, max_trials=trials, seed=seed + 21,
                                  anti_prob=0.25)
        consider_solution(state, h, c)
        h = state[1].copy()

    if time.time() < seg2_stop - 25.0:
        base = state[1].copy()
        if n_alt != len(base):
            h_int = interp_to(base, n_alt)
        else:
            h_int = base.copy()
        c_int = c5_value(h_int)
        h_sym = symmetrize_feasible(h_int)
        c_sym = c5_value(h_sym)

        if c_int <= c_sym:
            second_h = h_int
            second_c = c_int
        else:
            second_h = h_sym
            second_c = c_sym

        if shape_alt is not None and shape_alt_c <= second_c + 0.005:
            second_h = shape_alt.copy()
            second_c = shape_alt_c

        n_second = len(second_h)
        skip_second = (
            n_second == state[2] and
            second_c > state[0] + 1e-6
        )

        if not skip_second:
            if n_second <= 350:
                maxit2 = 110
            elif n_second <= 600:
                maxit2 = 70
            else:
                maxit2 = 45

            if time.time() < seg2_stop - 20.0:
                stop = min(seg2_stop - 6.0, time.time() + 120.0)
                h2, c2 = lbfgs_optimize(second_h, stop, [50.0, 500.0, 5000.0],
                                        [1e-3, 1e-2, 0.1], maxiter=maxit2)
                consider_solution(state, h2, c2)
                h2 = state[1].copy()

            if time.time() < seg2_stop - 25.0:
                stop = min(seg2_stop - 12.0, time.time() + 80.0)
                topK = 8 if len(h2) <= 400 else 6
                ppl = 45 if len(h2) <= 400 else 30
                h2, c2 = active_pair_search(h2, stop, topK=topK, pairs_per_lag=ppl,
                                            rates=(0.25, 0.5, 0.85), seed=seed + 31)
                consider_solution(state, h2, c2)
                h2 = state[1].copy()

            if time.time() < seg2_stop - 15.0:
                stop = min(seg2_stop - 6.0, time.time() + 100.0)
                h2, c2 = lbfgs_optimize(h2, stop, [50000.0, 500000.0, 2000000.0],
                                        [1.0, 10.0, 20.0], maxiter=maxit2)
                consider_solution(state, h2, c2)
                h2 = state[1].copy()

            if time.time() < seg2_stop - 3.0:
                stop = min(seg2_stop - 1.0, time.time() + 60.0)
                trials = 12000 if len(h2) <= 400 else 4500
                h2, c2 = random_pair_search(h2, stop, max_trials=trials,
                                            seed=seed + 41, anti_prob=0.25)
                consider_solution(state, h2, c2)
                h2 = state[1].copy()

    if time.time() < seg3_stop - 5.0:
        h = state[1].copy()
        n_h = len(h)
        if n_h <= 350:
            maxitf = 85
        elif n_h <= 600:
            maxitf = 55
        else:
            maxitf = 35

        if n_h <= 450 and time.time() < deadline - 140.0:
            stop = min(deadline - 130.0, time.time() + 60.0)
            h, c = slsqp_optimize(h, stop, alpha=1000000.0, maxiter=50)
            consider_solution(state, h, c)
            h = state[1].copy()

        if time.time() < deadline - 90.0:
            stop = min(deadline - 85.0, time.time() + 75.0)
            h, c = lbfgs_optimize(h, stop, [100000.0, 1000000.0, 5000000.0],
                                  [1.0, 10.0, 20.0], maxiter=maxitf)
            consider_solution(state, h, c)
            h = state[1].copy()

        if time.time() < deadline - 80.0:
            stop = min(deadline - 75.0, time.time() + 50.0)
            h, c = active_weight_descent(h, stop, K=12, max_steps=70)
            consider_solution(state, h, c)
            h = state[1].copy()

        if time.time() < deadline - 70.0:
            stop = min(deadline - 65.0, time.time() + 60.0)
            topK = 8 if len(h) <= 400 else 6
            ppl = 40 if len(h) <= 400 else 30
            h, c = active_pair_search(h, stop, topK=topK, pairs_per_lag=ppl,
                                      rates=(0.25, 0.5, 0.85), seed=seed + 51)
            consider_solution(state, h, c)
            h = state[1].copy()

        if time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
            stop = min(deadline - 1.0, time.time() + 65.0)
            trials = 25000 if len(h) <= 400 else 7000
            h, c = random_pair_search(h, stop, max_trials=trials,
                                      seed=seed + 61, anti_prob=0.25)
            consider_solution(state, h, c)
            h = state[1].copy()

    h = state[1]
    if h is None:
        h = make_triangle(300)
    h = project_feasible(h)
    c = c5_value(h)
    return h, float(c), len(h)
```
